Math.random() * width;\n      const posY = y ?? Math.random() * height;\n\n      const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n      const speedMagnitude = burst\n        ? (Math.random() * 8 + 4) * config.speed\n        : (Math.random() * 1.5 + 0.5) * config.speed;\n\n      return {\n        x: posX,\n        y: posY,\n        vx: Math.cos(angle) * speedMagnitude,\n        vy: Math.sin(angle) * speedMagnitude,\n        size: Math.random() * config.size + 1.2,\n        baseSize: config.size,\n        color: getRandomColor(),\n        alpha: Math.random() * 0.7 + 0.3,\n        life: burst ? Math.random() * 60 + 40 : 100,\n        maxLife: burst ? 100 : 100,\n      };\n    },\n    [config.speed, config.size, getRandomColor]\n  );\n\n  // パーティクル配列の初期化およびカウント同期\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const currentParticles = particlesRef.current;\n    const targetCount = config.count;\n\n    if (currentParticles.length < targetCount) {\n      const needed = targetCount - currentParticles.length;\n      for (let i = 0; i < needed; i++) {\n        currentParticles.push(createParticle());\n      }\n    } else if (currentParticles.length > targetCount) {\n      currentParticles.splice(targetCount);\n    }\n\n    // テーマ変更時は色も更新\n    const palette = THEME_PALETTES[config.theme] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n    currentParticles.forEach((p) => {\n      p.color = particlesRef.current[i % particlesRef.current.length];\n          const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n          const force = (Math.random() * 9 + 3) * config.speed;\n          p.x = burstX;\n         p.vx *= 0.96;\n            p.vy *= 0.96;\n            break;\n          }\n          case 'stream': {\n            // 流星トレイル: 右下への定常流 + ポインタ近傍での重力レンズ的偏向\n            p.vx += 0.35 * config.speed;\n            p.vy += 0.25 * config.speed;\n            if (dist < 260) {\n              const bendForce = (1 - dist / 260) * strength * 1.5 * config.speed;\n              p.vx += (dx /      default: {\n            // 追尾追従: しなやかな群れ動作\n            const pull = (strength * 40) / Math.max(dist, 40);\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * pull * 0.18 * config.speed;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * pull * 0.18 * config.speed;\n            // わずかなランダム揺らぎ\n            p.vx += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3;\n            p.vy += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3;\n            p.vx *= 0.95;\n            p.vy *= 0.95;\n            break;\n          }\n        }\n\n        // 速度制限\n        const maxVel = 14 * config.speed;\n        const currentSpeed = Math.sqrt(p.vx * p.vx + p.vy * p.vy);\n p.vy;\n\n        // 画面外ワープ / バウンス\n        if (p.x < -20) p.x = width + 20;\n        else if (p.x > width + 20) p.x = -20;\n        if (p.y < -20) p.y = height + 20;\n        else if (p.y > height + 20) p.y = -20;\n\n        // 描画\n        if (config.glow) {\n          ctx.shadowColor = p.color;\n        }\n\n        ctx.fillStyle = p.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha;\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.size, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 1.0;\n      animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n    };\n\n    animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n      if (animFrameIdRef.current) {\n e.clientY, 70);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    pointerRef.current.x = e.clientX;\n    pointerRef.current.y = e.clientY;\n    pointerRef.current.isActive = true;\n    pointerRef.current.lastActiveTime = Date.now();\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.lastActiveTime = Date.now();\n  };\n\n useState(false);\n  const [burstCount, setBurstCount] = useState(0);\n  const [fps, setFps] = useState(60);\n\n  const handleChangeConfig = useCallback((newConfig: Partial<ParticleConfig>) => {\n    setConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...newConfig }));\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleTriggerBurst = useCallback(() => {\n    setBurstCount((prev) => prev + 1);\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleTogglePanel = particlesRef.current.splice(0, particlesRef.current.length - maxAllowed);\n      }\n    },\n    []\n  );\n\n  // burstTriggerCount の外部変化（Spaceキー、爆発ボタン）を検知\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if createParticle]);\n\n  // Canvas初期化とリサイズ・描画ループ\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    let resizeRafId: number | null = null;\n\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      if (resizeRafId !== null) {\n        cancelAnimationFrame(resizeRafId);\n      }\n      resizeRafId = requestAnimationFrame(() => {\n        const container = containerRef.current || canvas.parentElement;\n        const width = container ? container.clientWidth : window.innerWidth;\n        const height = container ? container.clientHeight : window.innerHeight;\n        const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);\n\n        canvas.width = Math.floor(width * dpr);\n        canvas.height = Math.floor(height * dpr);\n        canvas.style.width = `${width}px`;\n        canvas.style.height = `${height}px`;\n\n        ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);\n        ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);\n\n        // 背景を完全クリア\n        ctx.fillStyle = '#070913';\n        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n        // 初期パーティクル生成（まだ空の場合）\n        if (particlesRef.current.length === 0) {\n          const initialList: ParticleItem[] = [];\n          for (let i = 0; i < configRef.current.count; i++) {\n            initialList.push(createParticle(width, height));\n          }\n          particlesRef.current = initialList;\n        }\n      });\n    };\n\n    handleResize();\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n    // 描画メインループ\n    const renderLoop = (time: number) => {\n      if (!lastTimeRef.current) {\n        lastTimeRef.current = time;\n        fpsLastTimeRef.current = time;\n      }\n\n      // デルタタイム算出（秒単位、上限0.1sでブラウザタブ切り替え時のスパイク防止）\n      const dt = Math.min((time - lastTimeRef.current) / 1000, 0.1);\n      lastTimeRef.current = time;\n\n      // FPS計測\n      fpsFrameCountRef.current++;\n      if (time - fpsLastTimeRef.current >= 1000) {\n        const calculatedFps = Math.round(\n          (fpsFrameCountRef.current * 1000) / (time - fpsLastTimeRef.current)\n        );\n        if (onFpsUpdate) {\n          onFpsUpdate(calculatedFps);\n        }\n        fpsFrameCountRef.current = 0;\n        fpsLastTimeRef.current = time;\n      }\n\n      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n      const width = rect.width;\n      const height = rect.height;\n\n      const currentConfig = configRef.current;\n      const pointer = pointerRef.current;\n\n      // トレイル（残像）フェード描画\n      // trailLength: 0.05(長い軌跡) 〜 0.5(クリアな軌跡)\n      const fadeAlpha = Math.max(0.04, Math.min(0.6, currentConfig.trailLength));\n      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(7, 9, 19, ${fadeAlpha})`;\n      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n      // 自律動作時の仮想ターゲット座標算出（ポインタ非操作時）\n      const timeSec = time * 0.001;\n      let targetX = pointer.x;\n      let targetY = pointer.y;\n\n      const isPointerInactive =\n        !pointer.isActive && time - pointer.lastActiveTime > 2500;\n      if (isPointerInactive) {\n        const radiusX = width * 0.35;\n        const radiusY = height * 0.3;\n        targetX = width / 2 + Math.cos(timeSec * 0.8) * radiusX;\n        targetY = height / 2 + Math.sin(timeSec * 1.2) * radiusY;\n      }\n\n      const speedFactor = currentConfig.speed;\n      const attraction = currentConfig.attractionStrength;\n      const mode = currentConfig.mode;\n      const particles = particlesRef.current;\n\n      for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n        const p = particles[i];\n\n        const dx = targetX - p.x;\n        const dy = targetY - p.y;\n        const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n        const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq) || 1;\n\n        // モード別物理演算\n        switch (mode) {\n          case 'follow': {\n            // スムーズな引力追尾 + わずかな周回力\n            const force = (Math.min(dist, 400) / 400) * attraction * 90;\n            const nx = dx / dist;\n            const ny = dy / dist;\n            p.vx += (nx * force - p.vx * 1.5) * dt * speedFactor;\n            p.vy += (ny * force - p.vy * 1.5) * dt * speedFactor;\n            break;\n          }\n          case 'vortex': {\n            // 渦潮・ブラックホール引力（接線方向のトルク + 中心引力）\n            const force = Math.max(0.1, 1 - dist / (width * 0.6)) * attraction * 140;\n            const nx = dx / dist;\n            const ny = dy / dist;\n            const tangentX = -ny;\n            const tangentY = nx;\n\n            p.vx += (nx * force * 0.6 + tangentX * force * 1.2 - p.vx * 1.2) * dt * speedFactor;\n            p.vy += (ny * force * 0.6 + tangentY * force * 1.2 - p.vy * 1.2) * dt * speedFactor;\n            break;\n          }\n          case 'explode': {\n            // 接触時に外側へ強く跳ね返る反発力場\n            const radius = 220;\n            if (dist < radius) {\n              const repel = ((radius - dist) / radius) * attraction * 280;\n              p.vx -= (dx / dist) * repel * dt * speedFactor;\n              p.vy -= (dy / dist) * repel * dt * speedFactor;\n            } else {\n              // 遠くではゆっくり中心へ回帰\n              p.vx += ((dx / dist) * 20 - p.vx * 0.5) * dt * speedFactor;\n              p.vy += ((dy / dist) * 20 - p.vy * 0.5) * dt * speedFactor;\n            }\n            break;\n          }\n          case 'stream': {\n            // 一方向（右斜め下）に流れる宇宙線ストリーム + ポインタ近接時の重力レンズ湾曲\n            p.vx += (70 * speedFactor - p.vx * 0.6) * dt;\n            p.vy += (40 * speedFactor - p.vy * 0.6) * dt;\n\n            if (dist < 300) {\n              const bend = (1 - dist / 300) * attraction * 120;\n              p.vx += (dx / dist) * bend * dt;\n              p.vy += (dy / dist) * bend * dt;\n            }\n            break;\n          }\n        }\n\n        // 速度上限の設定\n        const maxV = 600 * speedFactor;\n        const currentV = Math.sqrt(p.vx * p.vx + p.vy * p.vy);\n        if (currentV > maxV) {\n          p.vx = (p.vx / currentV) * maxV;\n          p.vy = (p.vy / currentV) * maxV;\n        }\n\n        // 座標更新\n        p.x += p.vx * dt;\n        p.y += p.vy * dt;\n\n        // 画面外ワープ・跳ね返り\n        const margin = 20;\n        if (mode === 'stream') {\n          if (p.x > width + margin) p.x = -margin;\n          if (p.x < -margin) p.x = width + margin;\n          if (p.y > height + margin) p.y = -margin;\n          if (p.y < -margin) p.y = height + margin;\n        } else {\n          if (p.x < 0) {\n            p.x = 0;\n            p.vx *= -0.7;\n          } else if (p.x > width) {\n            p.x = width;\n            p.vx *= -0.7;\n          }\n          if (p.y < 0) {\n            p.y = 0;\n            p.vy *= -0.7;\n          } else if (p.y > height) {\n            p.y = height;\n            p.vy *= -0.7;\n          }\n        }\n\n        p.life += dt * 60;\n        if (p.life > p.maxLife && particles.length > currentConfig.count) {\n          particles.splice(i, 1);\n          continue;\n        }\n\n        // パーティクル描画\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.size, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n\n        if (currentConfig.glow) {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = p.size * 2.5;\n          ctx.shadowColor = p.color;\n        } else {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n        }\n\n        ctx.fillStyle = p.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha;\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 1.0;\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n\n      animationFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(renderLoop);\n    };\n\n    animationFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(renderLoop);\n\n    return () => {\n      if (resizeRafId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(resizeRafId);\n      if (animationFrameIdRef.current !== null) {\n        cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameIdRef.current);\n      }\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n    };\n  }, [createParticle, onFpsUpdate]);\n\n  // ポインタ・タッチイベントハンドラ\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n    pointerRef.current = {\n      x: e.clientX - rect.left,\n      y: e.clientY - rect.top,\n      isActive: true,\n      lastActiveTime: performance.now(),\n    };\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const px = e.clientX - rect.left;\n    const py = e.clientY - rect.top;\n    pointerRef.current = {\n      x: px,\n      y: py,\n      isActive: true,\n      lastActiveTime: performance.now(),\n    };\n    triggerBurstAt(px, py, 60);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.isActive = false;\n    pointerRef.current.lastActiveTime = performance.now();\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.isActive = false;\n    pointerRef.current.lastActiveTime = performance.now();\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div ref={containerRef} className=\"canvas-container\">\n      <canvas\n        ref={canvasRef}\n        className=\"particle-canvas\"\n        onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}\n        onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}\n        onPointerUp={handlePointerUp}\n        onPointerLeave={handlePointerLeave}\n        onPointerCancel={handlePointerLeave}\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default ParticleCanvas;","language":"tsx"}

   size: 2.0,\n      attractionStrength: 1.0,\n      trailLength: 0.3,\n      glow: true,\n    },\n  },\n  {\n    id: 'neon-burst',\n    name: 'ネオンエクスプロージョン',\n    description: 0.18,\n      glow: true,\n    },\n  },\n];","language":"typescript"},{"path":"src/components/ParticleCanvas.tsx","content":"import React, { useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';\nimport { ParticleConfig, ParticleItem, PointerState } from '../types';\nimport { THEME_PALETTES } from '../constants';\n\ninterface ParticleCanvasProps {\n  config: ParticleConfig;\n  burstTriggerCount: number;\n  onFpsUpdate?: (fps: number) => void;\n}\n\nconst ParticleCanvas: React.FC<ParticleCanvasProps> = ({\n  config,\n  burstTriggerCount,\n  onFpsUpdate,\n}) => {\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n\n  // アニメーションおよびパーティクル状態への参照\n  const particlesRef = useRef<ParticleItem[]>([]);\n  const pointerRef = useRef<PointerState>({\n    x: 0,\n    y: 0,\n    isActive: false,\n    lastActiveTime: 0,\n  });\n  const configRef = useRef<ParticleConfig>(config);\n  configRef.current = config;\n\n  const animationFrameIdRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n  const lastTimeRef = useRef<number>(0);\n  const fpsFrameCountRef = useRef<number>(0);\n  const fpsLastTimeRef = useRef<number>(0);\n  const prevBurstTriggerRef = useRef<number>(burstTriggerCount);\n\n  // パーティクル初期化・再生成ヘルパー\n  const createParticle = useCallback(\n    (\n      width: number,\n      height: number,\n      originX?: number,\n      originY?: number,\n      customSpeed?: number\n    ): ParticleItem => {\n      const palette = THEME_PALETTES[configRef.current.theme] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n      const color = palette[Math.floor(Math.random() * palette.length)];\n      const baseSize = Math.max(0.8, configRef.current.size * (0.6 + Math.random() * 0.8));\n\n      const x = originX !== undefined ? originX : Math.random() * width;\n      const y = originY !== undefined ? originY : Math.random() * height;\n\n      const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n      const velocityMagnitude =\n        customSpeed !== undefined\n          ? customSpeed\n          : (0.4 + Math.random() * 1.2) * configRef.current.speed * 60;\n\n      return {\n        x,\n        y,\n        vx: Math.cos(angle) * velocityMagnitude,\n        vy: Math.sin(angle) * velocityMagnitude,\n        size: baseSize,\n        baseSize,\n        color,\n        alpha: 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.7,\n        life: 0,\n        maxLife: 200 + Math.random() * 300,\n      };\n    },\n    []\n  );\n\n  // 指定座標でのエネルギー爆散生成\n  const triggerBurstAt = useCallback(\n    (cx: number, cy: number, count: number = 70) => {\n      if (!canvasRef.current) return;\n      const rect = canvasRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();\n      const palette = THEME_PALETTES[configRef.current.theme] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n\n      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n        const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n        const speed = (2 + Math.random() * 8) * configRef.current.speed * 60;\n        const baseSize = configRef.current.size * (1.0 + Math.random() * 1.5);\n\n        particlesRef.current.push({\n          x: cx,\n          y: cy,\n          vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,\n          vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n          size: baseSize,\n          baseSize,\n          color: palette[Math.floor(Math.random() * palette.length)],\n          alpha: 1.0,\n          life: 0,\n          maxLife: 80 + Math.random() * 60,\n        });\n      }\n\n      // 粒子過多防止の安全策\n      const maxAllowed = configRef.current.count + 200;\n      if (particlesRef.current.length > maxAllowed) {\n        particlesRef.current.splice(0, particlesRef.current.length - maxAllowed);\n      }\n    },\n    []\n  );\n\n  // burstTriggerCount の外部変化（Spaceキー、爆発ボタン）を検知\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (burstTriggerCount > prevBurstTriggerRef.current) {\n      prevBurstTriggerRef.current = burstTriggerCount;\n      if (canvasRef.current) {\n        const rect = canvasRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();\n        const burstX = pointerRef.current.isActive\n          ? pointerRef.current.x\n          : rect.width / 2;\n        const burstY = pointerRef.current.isActive\n          ? pointerRef.current.y\n          : rect.height / 2;\n        triggerBurstAt(burstX, burstY, 100);\n      }\n    }\n  }, [burstTriggerCount, triggerBurstAt]);\n\n  // パーティクル配列の要素数とテーマ色を同期\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!canvasRef.current) return;\n    const rect = canvasRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const currentParticles = particlesRef.current;\n    const targetCount = config.count;\n\n    if (currentParticles.length < targetCount) {\n      const diff = targetCount - currentParticles.length;\n      for (let i = 0; i < diff; i++) {\n        currentParticles.push(createParticle(rect.width, rect.height));\n      }\n    } else if (currentParticles.length > targetCount) {\n      particlesRef.current = currentParticles.slice(0, targetCount);\n    }\n\n    // テーマ変更時に色を再割り当て\n    const palette = THEME_PALETTES[config.theme] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n    particlesRef.current.forEach((p) => {\n      p.color = palette[Math.floor(Math.random() * palette.length)];\n      p.baseSize = Math.max(0.8, config.size * (0.6 + Math.random() * 0.8));\n    });\n  }, [config.count, config.theme, config.size, createParticle]);\n\n  // Canvas初期化とリサイズ・描画ループ\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    let resizeRafId: number | null = null;\n\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      if (resizeRafId !== null) {\n        cancelAnimationFrame(resizeRafId);\n      }\n      resizeRafId = requestAnimationFrame(() => {\n        const container = containerRef.current || canvas.parentElement;\n        const width = container ? container.clientWidth : window.innerWidth;\n        const height = container ? container.clientHeight : window.innerHeight;\n        const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);\n\n        canvas.width = Math.floor(width * dpr);\n        canvas.height = Math.floor(height * dpr);\n        canvas.style.width = `${width}px`;\n        canvas.style.height = `${height}px`;\n\n        ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);\n        ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);\n\n        // 背景を完全クリア\n        ctx.fillStyle = '#070913';\n        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n        // 初期パーティクル生成（まだ空の場合）\n        if (particlesRef.current.length === 0) {\n          const initialList: ParticleItem[] = [];\n          for (let i = 0; i < configRef.current.count; i++) {\n            initialList.push(createParticle(width, height));\n          }\n          particlesRef.current = initialList;\n        }\n      });\n    };\n\n    handleResize();\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n    // 描画メインループ\n    const renderLoop = (time: number) => {\n      if (!lastTimeRef.current) {\n        lastTimeRef.current = time;\n        fpsLastTimeRef.current = time;\n      }\n\n      // デルタタイム算出（秒単位、上限0.1sでブラウザタブ切り替え時のスパイク防止）\n      const dt = Math.min((time - lastTimeRef.current) / 1000, 0.1);\n      lastTimeRef.current = time;\n\n      // FPS計測\n      fpsFrameCountRef.current++;\n      if (time - fpsLastTimeRef.current >= 1000) {\n        const calculatedFps = Math.round(\n          (fpsFrameCountRef.current * 1000) / (time - fpsLastTimeRef.current)\n        );\n        if (onFpsUpdate) {\n          onFpsUpdate(calculatedFps);\n        }\n        fpsFrameCountRef.current = 0;\n        fpsLastTimeRef.current = time;\n      }\n\n      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n      const width = rect.width;\n      const height = rect.height;\n\n      const currentConfig = configRef.current;\n      const pointer = pointerRef.current;\n\n      // トレイル（残像）フェード描画\n      // trailLength: 0.05(長い軌跡) 〜 0.5(クリアな軌跡)\n      const fadeAlpha = Math.max(0.04, Math.min(0.6, currentConfig.trailLength));\n      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(7, 9, 19, ${fadeAlpha})`;\n      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n      // 自律動作時の仮想ターゲット座標算出（ポインタ非操作時）\n      const timeSec = time * 0.001;\n      let targetX = pointer.x;\n      let targetY = pointer.y;\n\n      const isPointerInactive =\n        !pointer.isActive && time - pointer.lastActiveTime > 2500;\n      if (isPointerInactive) {\n        const radiusX = width * 0.35;\n        const radiusY = height * 0.3;\n        targetX = width / 2 + Math.cos(timeSec * 0.8) * radiusX;\n        targetY = height / 2 + Math.sin(timeSec * 1.2) * radiusY;\n      }\n\n      const speedFactor = currentConfig.speed;\n      const attraction = currentConfig.attractionStrength;\n      const mode = currentConfig.mode;\n      const particles = particlesRef.current;\n\n      for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n        const p = particles[i];\n\n        const dx = targetX - p.x;\n        const dy = targetY - p.y;\n        const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n        const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq) || 1;\n\n        // モード別物理演算\n        switch (mode) {\n          case 'follow': {\n            // スムーズな引力追尾 + わずかな周回力\n            const force = (Math.min(dist, 400) / 400) * attraction * 90;\n            const nx = dx / dist;\n            const ny = dy / dist;\n            p.vx += (nx * force - p.vx * 1.5) * dt * speedFactor;\n            p.vy += (ny * force - p.vy * 1.5) * dt * speedFactor;\n            break;\n          }\n          case 'vortex': {\n            // 渦潮・ブラックホール引力（接線方向のトルク + 中心引力）\n            const force = Math.max(0.1, 1 - dist / (width * 0.6)) * attraction * 140;\n            const nx = dx / dist;\n            const ny = dy / dist;\n            const tangentX = -ny;\n            const tangentY = nx;\n\n            p.vx += (nx * force * 0.6 + tangentX * force * 1.2 - p.vx * 1.2) * dt * speedFactor;\n            p.vy += (ny * force * 0.6 + tangentY * force * 1.2 - p.vy * 1.2) * dt * speedFactor;\n            break;\n          }\n          case 'explode': {\n            // 接触時に外側へ強く跳ね返る反発力場\n            const radius = 220;\n            if (dist < radius) {\n              const repel = ((radius - dist) / radius) * attraction * 280;\n              p.vx -= (dx / dist) * repel * dt * speedFactor;\n              p.vy -= (dy / dist) * repel * dt * speedFactor;\n            } else {\n              // 遠くではゆっくり中心へ回帰\n              p.vx += ((dx / dist) * 20 - p.vx * 0.5) * dt * speedFactor;\n              p.vy += ((dy / dist) * 20 - p.vy * 0.5) * dt * speedFactor;\n            }\n            break;\n          }\n          case 'stream': {\n            // 一方向（右斜め下）に流れる宇宙線ストリーム + ポインタ近接時の重力レンズ湾曲\n            p.vx += (70 * speedFactor - p.vx * 0.6) * dt;\n            p.vy += (40 * speedFactor - p.vy * 0.6) * dt;\n\n            if (dist < 300) {\n              const bend = (1 - dist / 300) * attraction * 120;\n              p.vx += (dx / dist) * bend * dt;\n              p.vy += (dy / dist) * bend * dt;\n            }\n            break;\n          }\n        }\n\n        // 速度上限の設定\n        const maxV = 600 * speedFactor;\n        const currentV = Math.sqrt(p.vx * p.vx + p.vy * p.vy);\n        if (currentV > maxV) {\n          p.vx = (p.vx / currentV) * maxV;\n          p.vy = (p.vy / currentV) * maxV;\n        }\n\n        // 座標更新\n        p.x += p.vx * dt;\n        p.y += p.vy * dt;\n\n        // 画面外ワープ・跳ね返り\n        const margin = 20;\n        if (mode === 'stream') {\n          if (p.x > width + margin) p.x = -margin;\n          if (p.x < -margin) p.x = width + margin;\n          if (p.y > height + margin) p.y = -margin;\n          if (p.y < -margin) p.y = height + margin;\n        } else {\n          if (p.x < 0) {\n            p.x = 0;\n            p.vx *= -0.7;\n          } else if (p.x > width) {\n            p.x = width;\n            p.vx *= -0.7;\n          }\n          if (p.y < 0) {\n            p.y = 0;\n            p.vy *= -0.7;\n          } else if (p.y > height) {\n            p.y = height;\n            p.vy *= -0.7;\n          }\n        }\n\n        p.life += dt * 60;\n        if (p.life > p.maxLife && particles.length > currentConfig.count) {\n          particles.splice(i, 1);\n          continue;\n        }\n\n        // パーティクル描画\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.size, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n\n        if (currentConfig.glow) {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = p.size * 2.5;\n          ctx.shadowColor = p.color;\n        } else {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n        }\n\n        ctx.fillStyle = p.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha;\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 1.0;\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n\n cancelAnimationFrame(resizeRafId);\n      if (animationFrameIdRef.current !== null) {\n        cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameIdRef.current);\n      }\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n    };\n  }, [createParticle, onFpsUpdate]);\n\n  // ポインタ・タッチイベントハンドラ\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const canvas = pointerRef.current.lastActiveTime = performance.now();\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.isActive = false;\n    pointerRef.current.lastActiveTime = performance.now();\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div ref={containerRef} className=\"canvas-container\">\n      <canvas\n        ref={canvasRef}\n        className=\"particle-canvas\"\n
